## Narration 1226

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The Arabic source reports past conduct and related revelation. The final explanatory comment remains context; unnamed persons stay unresolved.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ ثَوْرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ الدِّيلِيِّ، أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ، كَانَ يُطَلِّقُ امْرَأَتَهُ ثُمَّ يُرَاجِعُهَا وَلاَ حَاجَةَ لَهُ بِهَا وَلاَ يُرِيدُ إِمْسَاكَهَا كَيْمَا يُطَوِّلَ بِذَلِكَ عَلَيْهَا الْعِدَّةَ لِيُضَارَّهَا فَأَنْزَلَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏وَلاَ تُمْسِكُوهُنَّ ضِرَارًا لِتَعْتَدُوا وَمَنْ يَفْعَلْ ذَلِكَ فَقَدْ ظَلَمَ نَفْسَهُ‏}‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1240]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The source reports a past harmful divorce practice and a related revelation. The final reference and explanatory warning remain context; it is not labelled a Prophetic saying.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Thawr ibn Zayd ad-Dili that Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, sent down about a man who divorced his wife and then returned to her while he had no need of her and did not mean to keep her so as to make the idda period long for her by that in order to do her harm, &quot;Do not retain them by force, to transgress. Whoever does that has wronged himself.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1240]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ठौर बिन ज़ैद डैली से रिवायत है के अगले ज़माने में लोग अपनी औरतों को तलाक देते थे फिर राजत कर लेते थे और उनके रखने की नीयत न होती थी आयत उतरी: &#x27;औरतों को ज़रर पहुँचाने के लिए मत रोक रखा ता&#x27;अला लोगों को यह नसीहत करता है.&#x27; सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सुलैमान बिन यसर से सवाल हुआ कि जो शख्स नशे में मस्त हो और तलाक़ दे उसका क्या हुकुम है? दोनों ने कहा के तलाक़ पद जायेगी और वह नशे में मार डाले किसी को तो मारा जायेगा. इमाम मलिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक यही हुकुम है. सईद बिन मुसय्यब कहते थे जब खाविंद जोरू को नान-ो-नफ़ाक़ाह न दे सके तो तफ़रीक़ कर दी जाएगी. इमाम मलिक ने कहा मैंने अपने शहर के आलिमों को इसी पर पाया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">ثور بن زید دیلی سے روایت ہے کہ اگلے زمانہ میں لوگ اپنی عورتوں کو طلاق دیتے تھے پھر رجعت کر لیتے تھے اور ان کے رکھنے کی نیت نہ ہوتی تھی تاکہ ان کی عدت بٹھ جائے اور ان کو ضرر پہنچے تب اللہ تعالیٰ نے یہ آیت اتاری عورتوں کو ضرر پہنچانے کے لئے مت روک رکھو جو ایسا کرے گا اس نے اپنے نفس پر ظلم کیا</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے سوال ہوا کہ جو شخص نشے میں مست ہو اور طلاق دے اس کا کیا حکم ہے دونوں نے کہا کہ طلاق پڑ جائے گی اور وہ نشے میں مار ڈالے کسی کو تو مارا جائے گا ۔</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 3</p>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب کہتے تھے جب خاوند جورو کو نان نفقہ نہ دے سکے تو تفریق کر دی جائے گی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1240]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Thaur bin Zaid Daili se riwayat hai ke agle zamane mein log apni auraton ko talaq dete thay phir rajat kar lete thay aur unke rakhne ki niyat na hoti thi, taake unki iddat badh jaye aur unko zarar (nuqsan) pahunche. Tab Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne yeh ayat utari: &#x27;Auraton ko zarar pahunchane ke liye mat rok rakho, jo aisa karega usne apne nafs par zulm kiya, Allah Ta&#x27;ala logon ko yeh nasihat karta hai.&#x27; Saeed bin Musayyab aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se sawal hua ke jo shakhs nashe mein mast ho aur talaq de uska kya hukum hai? Dono ne kaha ke talaq pad jayegi aur woh nashe mein maar dale kisi ko to mara jayega. Imam Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek yahi hukum hai. Saeed bin Musayyab kehte thay jab khawind joru ko naan-o-nafaqah na de sake to tafreeq kar di jayegi. Imam Malik ne kaha maine apne shehar ke aalimon ko isi par paya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1240" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، سُئِلاَ عَنْ طَلاَقِ السَّكْرَانِ، فَقَالاَ إِذَا طَلَّقَ السَّكْرَانُ جَازَ طَلاَقُهُ وَإِنْ قَتَلَ قُتِلَ بِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab and Sulayman ibn Yasar were asked about a man who divorced when he was drunk. They said, &quot;When a drunk man divorces, his divorce is allowed. If he kills, he is killed for it.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1241]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्होंने सुना था के सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब और सुलईमान इब्न यासर से उस शख़्स के बारे में पूछा गया जिस ने नशे की हालत में तलाक़ दी। उन्होंने कहा: जब नशे में धुत आदमी तलाक़ दे तो उस की तलाक़ वाक़य हो जाती है, और अगर वो क़त्ल करे तो उसे उस के बदले क़त्ल किया जाए गा। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: हमारे हाँ इसी पर अमल है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا تھا کہ سعید بن المسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے اس شخص کے بارے میں پوچھا گیا جس نے نشے کی حالت میں طلاق دی۔ انہوں نے کہا: جب نشے میں دھت آدمی طلاق دے تو اس کی طلاق واقع ہو جاتی ہے، اور اگر وہ قتل کرے تو اسے اس کے بدلے قتل کیا جائے گا۔ امام مالک نے کہا: ہمارے ہاں اسی پر عمل ہے۔</p>

Unhon ne kaha: Jab nashe mein dhut aadmi talaq de to us ki talaq waqay ho jati hai, aur agar wo qatl kare to use us ke badle qatl kiya jaye ga. Imam Malik ne kaha: Hamare haan isi par amal hai.</p>

## Narration 1228

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، كَانَ يَقُولُ إِذَا لَمْ يَجِدِ الرَّجُلُ مَا يُنْفِقُ عَلَى امْرَأَتِهِ فُرِّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ أَدْرَكْتُ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;If a man does not find the means to spend on his wife, they are to be separated . &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1242]</p>

## Narration 1229

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1243)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ رَبِّهِ بْنِ سَعِيدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سُئِلَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ وَأَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ الْحَامِلِ يُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ آخِرَ الأَجَلَيْنِ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ إِذَا وَلَدَتْ فَقَدْ حَلَّتْ ‏.‏ فَدَخَلَ أَبُو سَلَمَةَ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَلَى أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَتْ أُمُّ سَلَمَةَ وَلَدَتْ سُبَيْعَةُ الأَسْلَمِيَّةُ بَعْدَ وَفَاةِ زَوْجِهَا بِنِصْفِ شَهْرٍ فَخَطَبَهَا رَجُلاَنِ أَحَدُهُمَا شَابٌّ وَالآخَرُ كَهْلٌ فَحَطَّتْ إِلَى الشَّابِّ فَقَالَ الشَّيْخُ لَمْ تَحِلِّي بَعْدُ ‏.‏ وَكَانَ أَهْلُهَا غَيَبًا وَرَجَا إِذَا جَاءَ أَهْلُهَا أَنْ يُؤْثِرُوهُ بِهَا فَجَاءَتْ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ حَلَلْتِ فَانْكِحِي مَنْ شِئْتِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1243</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1243 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdu Rabbih ibn Said ibn Qays that Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman said that Abdullah ibn Abbas and Abu Hurayra were asked when a pregnant woman whose husband had died could remarry. Ibn Abbas said, &quot;At the end of two periods.&quot; Abu Hurayra said, &quot;When she gives birth, she is free to marry.&quot; Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman visited Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and asked her about it Umm Salama said, &#x27;&#x27;Subaya al-Aslamiya gave birth half a month after the death of her husband, and two men asked to marry her. One was young and the other was old. She preferred the young man and so the older man said, &#x27;You are not free to marry yet.&#x27; Her family were away and he hoped that when her family came, they would give her to him. She went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he said, &#x27;You are free to marry, so marry whomever you wish</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1243]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सलामा बिन अब्दुर-रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास और अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल हुआ के हामिला औरत का खावंद अगर मर जाए तो वो किस हिसाब से इद्दत करे। इब्न अब्बास ने कहा के दोनों इद्दतों में से जो इद्दत दूर हो उसको इख़्तियार करे और अबू हुरैरा ने कहा के वज़अ-ए-हम्ल तक इंतेज़ार करे। फिर अबू सलामा के पास गईं और उनसे जा कर पूछा, उन्होंने कहा के सुबैआ असलामिया अपने खावंद के मरने के बाद पंद्रह दिन में जनी, फिर दो शख़्सों ने उसको पैग़ाम भेजा, एक नौजवान था दूसरा ढेर। वो नौजवान की तरफ़ माइल हुई। ढेर ने कहा तेरी इद्दत ही अभी नहीं गुज़री, इस ख़्याल से के उसके अज़ीज़ वहाँ न थे, जब वो आएँगे तो शायद इस औरत को मेरी तरफ़ माइल कर दें। फिर सुबैआ रसूले अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आई और ये हाल बयान किया। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया तेरी इद्दत गुज़र गई, तू जिसे चाहे निकाह कर ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عباس اور ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سوال ہوا کہ حاملہ عورت کا خاوند اگر مر جائے تو وہ کس حساب سے عدت کرے ابن عباس نے کہا کہ دونوں عدتوں میں سے جو عدت دور ہو اس کو اختیار کرے اور ابوہریرہ نے کہا کہ وضع حمل تک انتطار کرے پھر ابوسلمہ کے پاس گئیں اور ان سے جا کر پوچھا انہوں نے کہا کہ سبیعہ اسلمیہ اپنے خاوند کے مرنے کے بعد پندرہ دن میں جنی پھر دو شخصوں نے اس کو پیام بھیجا ایک نوجوان تھا دوسر ادھیڑ وہ نوجوان کی طرف مائل ہوئی ادھیڑ نے کہا تیری عدت ہی ابھی نہیں گزری اس خیال سے کہ اس کے عزیز وہاں نہ تھے جب وہ آئیں گے تو شاید اس عورت کو میری طرف مائل کر دیں پھر سبیعہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آئی اور یہ حال بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا تیری عدت گرز گئی تو جس سے چاہے نکاح کر لے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Salamah bin Abdur-Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Abbas aur Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se sawal hua ke haamila aurat ka khawand agar mar jaye to wo kis hisab se iddat kare. Ibn Abbas ne kaha ke dono iddaton mein se jo iddat door ho usko ikhtiyar kare aur Abu Hurairah ne kaha ke waza-e-haml tak intezar kare. Phir Abu Salamah ke paas gayin aur unse ja kar poocha, unhone kaha ke Subai&#x27;a Aslamiyah apne khawand ke marne ke baad pandrah din mein jani, phir do shakhson ne usko paigham bheja, ek naujawan tha dusra dher. Wo naujawan ki taraf mail hui. Dher ne kaha teri iddat hi abhi nahi guzri, is khayal se ke uske azeez wahan na the, jab wo aayenge to shayad is aurat ko meri taraf mail kar den. Phir Subai&#x27;a Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aayi aur ye haal bayan kiya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya teri iddat guzar gayi, tu jisse chahe nikah kar le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1230

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1244)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1244]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ سُئِلَ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ، يُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا وَهِيَ حَامِلٌ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ إِذَا وَضَعَتْ حَمْلَهَا فَقَدْ حَلَّتْ ‏.‏ فَأَخْبَرَهُ رَجُلٌ مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ كَانَ عِنْدَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ قَالَ لَوْ وَضَعَتْ وَزَوْجُهَا عَلَى سَرِيرِهِ لَمْ يُدْفَنْ بَعْدُ لَحَلَّتْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar was asked about a woman whose husband died while she was pregnant, and he said, &quot;When she gives birth, she is free to marry.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">A man of the Ansar who was with him told him that Umar ibn al-Khattab had said, &quot;Had she given birth while her husband was still on his bed, unburied, she would be free to marry</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1244]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर से सवाल हुआ के अगर हामिला औरत का खावंद मर जाए तो उसकी इद्दत क्या है? अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा जब वो बच्चा जने, उसकी इद्दत पूरी हो गई। इतने में एक शख्स अंसारी ने कहा के हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब ने फरमाया अगर खावंद का जनाज़ा तख्त पर रखा हुआ हो और उसकी औरत बच्चा जने तो उसकी इद्दत गुज़र जाएगी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar se sawal hua ke agar haamila aurat ka khawand mar jaye to uski iddat kya hai? Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha jab wo bacha jane, uski iddat poori ho gayi. Itne mein ek shakhs Ansari ne kaha ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab ne farmaya agar khawand ka janaza takht par rakha hua ho aur uski aurat bacha jane to uski iddat guzar jayegi.</p>

## Narration 1231

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنِ الْمِسْوَرِ بْنِ مَخْرَمَةَ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ سُبَيْعَةَ الأَسْلَمِيَّةَ نُفِسَتْ بَعْدَ وَفَاةِ زَوْجِهَا بِلَيَالٍ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ حَلَلْتِ فَانْكِحِي مَنْ شِئْتِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1245" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Subaya al-Aslamiya in al-Miswar ibn Makhramas account. The complete field supplies childbirth shortly after the husbands death and the direct permission to marry.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn &#x27;Urwa from his father that al-Miswar ibn Makhrama told him that Subaya al-Aslamiya gave birth a few nights after the death of her husband. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to her, &quot;You are free to marry, so marry whomever you wish</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1245]</p>

## Narration 1232

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1246)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، وَأَبَا، سَلَمَةَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ اخْتَلَفَا فِي الْمَرْأَةِ تُنْفَسُ بَعْدَ وَفَاةِ زَوْجِهَا بِلَيَالٍ فَقَالَ أَبُو سَلَمَةَ إِذَا وَضَعَتْ مَا فِي بَطْنِهَا فَقَدْ حَلَّتْ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ آخِرَ الأَجَلَيْنِ ‏.‏ فَجَاءَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ فَقَالَ أَنَا مَعَ ابْنِ أَخِي ‏.‏ يَعْنِي أَبَا سَلَمَةَ فَبَعَثُوا كُرَيْبًا مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ إِلَى أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَسْأَلُهَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَجَاءَهُمْ فَأَخْبَرَهُمْ أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ وَلَدَتْ سُبَيْعَةُ الأَسْلَمِيَّةُ بَعْدَ وَفَاةِ زَوْجِهَا بِلَيَالٍ فَذَكَرَتْ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ حَلَلْتِ فَانْكِحِي مَنْ شِئْتِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Abdullah ibn Abbas and Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf differed on the question of a wornan who gave birth a few nights after the death of her husband. Abu Salama said, &quot;When she gives birth to the child she is carrying, she is free to marry.&quot; Ibn Abbas said, &quot;At the end of two periods.&quot; Abu Hurayra came and said, &quot;I am with my nephew&quot;, meaning Abu Salama. They sent Kurayb, a mawla of Abdullah ibn Abbas to Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, to ask her about it. He came back and told them that she had said that Subaya al-Aslamiya had given birth a few nights after the death of her husband, and she had brought the matter to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he had said, &quot;You are free to marry, so marry whomever you wish.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is how the people of knowledge here continue to act.&quot; 29.31 Widows Remaining in Their Houses until Free to Marry</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1246</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास और अबू सलमा बिन अब्दुर-रहमान ने उस औरत की इद्दत में इख्तिलाफ किया जो पंद्रह दिन के बाद अपने खावंद के मरने के बाद बच्चा जने। अबू सलमा ने कहा जब वो बच्चा जने तो उसकी इद्दत गुज़र गई और अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास ने कहा नहीं, दोनों इद्दों में जो दूर हो वहां तक इंतज़ार करे। इतने में अबू हुरैराह आए, उन्होंने कहा के मैं अपने भाई अबू सलमा के साथ हूँ। फिर इन सब लोगों ने इस मसले को पूछने के वास्ते हज़रत उम्मे-सलमा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास कुरैब को भेजा, जो अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास के मौला थे। उन्होंने कहा के सुबै&#x27;अ असलामिया अपने खावंद के मरने के बाद चंद रोज़ के बाद जनी, जब आन-हज़रत (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से बयान किया, आपने फरमाया तू हलाल हो गई, जिससे चाहे निकाह करे। कहा मालिक ने, हमारे नज़दीक यही हुक्म है और हमारे शहर के आलिम इसी मज़हब पर रहे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1246]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عباس اور ابوسلمہ بن عبدالرحمن نے اس عورت کی عدت میں اختلاف کیا جو پندرہ دن کے بعد اپنے خاوند کے مرنے کے بعد بچہ جنے ابوسلمہ نے کہا جب وہ بچہ جنے تو اس کی عدت گزر گئی اور عبداللہ بن عباس نے کہا نہیں دونوں عدتوں میں جو دور ہو وہاں تک انتظار کرے اتنے میں ابوہریرہ آئے انہوں نے کہا کہ میں اپنے بھائی ابوسلمہ کے ساتھ ہوں پھر ان سب لوگوں نے اس مسئلے کو پوچھنے کے واسطے حضرت ام سلمہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس کریب کو بھیجا جو عبداللہ بن عباس کے مولیٰ تھے انہوں نے کہا کہ سبیعہ اسلمیہ اپنے خاوند کے مرنے کے بعد چند روز کے بعد جنی جب آنحضرت صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے بیان کیا آپ نے فرمایا تو حلال ہوگئی جس سے چاہے نکاح کرے۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہی حکم ہے اور ہمارے شہر کے عالم اسی مذہب پر رہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Abbas aur Abu Salamah bin Abdur-Rahman ne us aurat ki iddat mein ikhtilaf kiya jo pandrah din ke baad apne khawand ke marne ke baad bacha jane. Abu Salamah ne kaha jab wo bacha jane to uski iddat guzar gayi aur Abdullah bin Abbas ne kaha nahi, dono iddaton mein jo door ho wahan tak intezar kare. Itne mein Abu Hurairah aaye, unhone kaha ke main apne bhai Abu Salamah ke saath hoon. Phir in sab logon ne is masle ko poochne ke waste Hazrat Umm-e-Salamah (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas Kuraib ko bheja, jo Abdullah bin Abbas ke maula the. Unhone kaha ke Subai&#x27;a Aslamiyah apne khawand ke marne ke baad chand roz ke baad jani, jab Aan-Hazrat (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se bayan kiya, aapne farmaya tu halal ho gayi, jisse chahe nikah kare. Kaha Malik ne, hamare nazdeek yahi hukm hai aur hamare shehar ke aalim isi mazhab par rahe.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1233

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1247)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ كَعْبِ بْنِ عُجْرَةَ، عَنْ عَمَّتِهِ، زَيْنَبَ بِنْتِ كَعْبِ بْنِ عُجْرَةَ أَنَّ الْفُرَيْعَةَ بِنْتَ مَالِكِ بْنِ سِنَانٍ، - وَهِيَ أُخْتُ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ - أَخْبَرَتْهَا أَنَّهَا، جَاءَتْ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم تَسْأَلُهُ أَنْ تَرْجِعَ إِلَى أَهْلِهَا فِي بَنِي خُدْرَةَ فَإِنَّ زَوْجَهَا خَرَجَ فِي طَلَبِ أَعْبُدٍ لَهُ أَبَقُوا حَتَّى إِذَا كَانُوا بِطَرَفِ الْقَدُومِ لَحِقَهُمْ فَقَتَلُوهُ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَسَأَلْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ أَرْجِعَ إِلَى أَهْلِي فِي بَنِي خُدْرَةَ فَإِنَّ زَوْجِي لَمْ يَتْرُكْنِي فِي مَسْكَنٍ يَمْلِكُهُ وَلاَ نَفَقَةَ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَانْصَرَفْتُ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنْتُ فِي الْحُجْرَةِ نَادَانِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَوْ أَمَرَ بِي فَنُودِيتُ لَهُ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ كَيْفَ قُلْتِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَرَدَّدْتُ عَلَيْهِ الْقِصَّةَ الَّتِي ذَكَرْتُ لَهُ مِنْ شَأْنِ زَوْجِي فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ امْكُثِي فِي بَيْتِكِ حَتَّى يَبْلُغَ الْكِتَابُ أَجَلَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَاعْتَدَدْتُ فِيهِ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا - قَالَتْ - فَلَمَّا كَانَ عُثْمَانُ بْنُ عَفَّانَ أَرْسَلَ إِلَىَّ فَسَأَلَنِي عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَأَخْبَرْتُهُ فَاتَّبَعَهُ وَقَضَى بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Said ibn Ishaq ibn Kab ibn Ujra from his paternal aunt, Zaynab bint Kab ibn Ujra that al-Furaya bint Malik ibn Sinan, the sister of Abu Said al-Khudri, informed her that she went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and asked to be able to return to her people among the Banu Khudra since her husband had gone out in search of some of his slaves who had run away and he had caught up with them near al-Qudum, (which is 6 miles from Madina), and they had killed him. She said, &quot;I asked the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, if I could return to my people in the Banu Khudra, as my husband had not left me in a dwelling which belonged to him, and had left me no maintenance. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said,&#x27;Yes.&#x27; So I left. When I was in the courtyard, the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, called me or summoned me, and I answered him. He said, &#x27;What did you say?&#x27; I repeated the story about my husband. He said, &#x27;Stay in your house until what is written reaches its term.&#x27; I did the idda in the house for four months and ten days.&quot; She added, &quot;When Uthman ibn Affan sent for me, I told him that, and he followed it and made decisions by it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैनब बिंत का&#x27;अब बिन उजराह से रिवायत है के फारी&#x27;अ बिंत मालिक बिन सिनान जो अबू सईद खुदरी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) की बहन हैं, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आईं और पूछा के मुझे अपने लोगों में जाने की इजाज़त है क्योंकि मेरे खावंद के चंद गुलाम भाग गए थे, वो उनको ढूँढने को निकले। जब कुदूम (एक मकाम है मदीना से सात मील पर) में पहुँची वहां गुलामों को पाया और गुलामों ने मेरे खावंद को मार डाला और मेरा खावंद मेरे लिए न कोई मकान ज़ात का छोड़ गया है न कुछ खर्च दे गया है, अगर आप कहिए तो मैं अपने कुनबे वालों में चली जाऊं। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया चली जा। जब मैं लौट कर चली, हुजरा के बाहर नहीं पहुँची थी के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पुकारा या किसी और ने आप के कहने पर पुकारा और मुझसे पूछा। मैंने सारा वाकिया बयान किया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया के इसी घर में रह यहां तक के इद्दत पूरी हो। मैंने इसी घर में इद्दत की चार महीने दस दिन तक। जब हज़रत उस्मान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) खलीफा हुए, उन्होंने मुझसे ये मसला पुछवा भेजा और इसी के मुवाफिक हुक्म किया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1247]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زینت بنت کعب بن عجرہ سے روایت ہے کہ فریعہ بنت مالک بن سنان جو ابوسعید خدری رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کی بہن ہیں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آئیں اور پوچھا کہ مجھے اپنے لوگوں میں جانے کی اجازت ہے کیونکہ میرے خاوند کے چند غلام بھاگ گئے تھے وہ ان کو ڈھونڈنے کو نکلے جب قدوم (ایک مقام ہے مدینہ سے سات میل پر) میں پہنچی وہاں غلاموں کو پایا اور غلاموں نے میرے خاوند کو مار ڈالا اور میرا خاوند میرے لئے نہ کوئی مکان ذات کا چھوڑ گیا ہے نہ کچھ خرچ دے گیا ہے اگر آپ کہئے تو میں اپنے کنبے والوں میں چلی جاؤں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا چلی جا جب میں لوٹ کر چلی حجرہ کے باہر نہیں پہنچی تھی کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پکارا یا کسی اور نے آپ کے کہنے پر پکارا اور مجھ سے پوچھا میں نے سارا واقعہ بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ اسی گھر میں رہ یہاں تک کہ عدت پوری ہو میں نے اسی گھر میں عدت کی چار مہینے دس تک جب حضرت عثمان رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ خلیہ ہوئے انہوں نے مجھ سے یہ مسئلہ پوچھوا بھیجا اور اسی کے موافق حکم کیا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1247]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zainab bint Ka&#x27;ab bin Ujrah se riwayat hai ke Fari&#x27;a bint Malik bin Sinan jo Abu Sa&#x27;eed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki behan hain, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aayin aur poocha ke mujhe apne logon mein jaane ki ijazat hai kyunke mere khawand ke chand ghulam bhaag gaye the, wo unko dhoondne ko nikle. Jab Qudoom (ek maqam hai Madina se saat meel par) mein pahunchi wahan ghulamon ko paya aur ghulamon ne mere khawand ko maar dala aur mera khawand mere liye na koi makan zaat ka chhod gaya hai na kuch kharch de gaya hai, agar aap kahiye to main apne kunbay walon mein chali jaaun. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya chali ja. Jab main laut kar chali, hujra ke bahar nahi pahunchi thi ke aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pukara ya kisi aur ne aap ke kehne par pukara aur mujhse poocha. Maine sara waqiya bayan kiya, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke isi ghar mein reh yahan tak ke iddat poori ho. Maine isi ghar mein iddat ki chaar mahine das din tak. Jab Hazrat Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) khalifa hue, unhone mujhse ye masla poochwa bheja aur isi ke muwafiq hukm kiya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، كَانَ يَرُدُّ الْمُتَوَفَّى عَنْهُنَّ أَزْوَاجُهُنَّ مِنَ الْبَيْدَاءِ يَمْنَعُهُنَّ الْحَجَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki from Amr ibn Shuayb from Said ibn al-Musayyab that Umar ibn al-Khattab sent back widows from the desert and prevented them from doing the hajj</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1248]</p>

## Narration 1235

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1249)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ السَّائِبَ بْنَ خَبَّابٍ، تُوُفِّيَ وَإِنَّ امْرَأَتَهُ جَاءَتْ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَذَكَرَتْ لَهُ وَفَاةَ زَوْجِهَا وَذَكَرَتْ لَهُ حَرْثًا لَهُمْ بِقَنَاةَ وَسَأَلَتْهُ هَلْ يَصْلُحُ لَهَا أَنْ تَبِيتَ فِيهِ فَنَهَاهَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَكَانَتْ تَخْرُجُ مِنَ الْمَدِينَةِ سَحَرًا فَتُصْبِحُ فِي حَرْثِهِمْ فَتَظَلُّ فِيهِ يَوْمَهَا ثُمَّ تَدْخُلُ الْمَدِينَةَ إِذَا أَمْسَتْ فَتَبِيتُ فِي بَيْتِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source episode is one report in two exact passages around the explicit place gloss; no words are rewritten. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Saqd that he had heard that as-Sa&#x27;ib ibn Khabbab died, and his wife went to Abdullah ibn Umar and mentioned to him that her husband had died and mentioned some land which they had at Qanah,</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr">, and asked him if it would be alright for her to stay overnight there. He forbade her to do so. So, she went out before dawn from Madina and spent the whole day on their land, but when evening came, she spent the night in her house</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1249 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सा&#x27;इब बिन खब्बाब का इंतकाल हो गया तो उनकी बीवी अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर के पास आईं और अपने खावंद का मरना बयान किया और कहा के मेरी कुछ खेती है, चाहे अगर आप इजाज़त दीजिए तो मैं शब को वहां रहा करूँ। उन्होंने इससे मना किया तो वो मदीना से सुबह को जातीं, दिन भर अपने खेत में रहतीं और सारा दिन वहां काटिन, शाम को फिर मदीना में आ जातीं और रात फिर अपने घर में बसर करतीं।</p>

Unhone isse mana kiya to wo Madina se subah ko jaatin, din bhar apne khet mein rehtin aur sara din wahan kaatin, sham ko phir Madina mein aa jaatin aur raat phir apne ghar mein basar kartin.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي الْمَرْأَةِ الْبَدَوِيَّةِ يُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا إِنَّهَا تَنْتَوِي حَيْثُ انْتَوَى أَهْلُهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: Hisham ibn Urwa (unresolved). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Hisham ibn Urwa said about a Bedouin woman whose husband died, that she was to stay where her people stayed.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1250]</p>

## Narration 1238

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1252)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1252]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1252" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1252</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1252 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَمِعْتُ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، يَقُولُ إِنَّ يَزِيدَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ فَرَّقَ بَيْنَ رِجَالٍ وَبَيْنَ نِسَائِهِمْ وَكُنَّ أُمَّهَاتِ أَوْلاَدِ رِجَالٍ هَلَكُوا فَتَزَوَّجُوهُنَّ بَعْدَ حَيْضَةٍ أَوْ حَيْضَتَيْنِ فَفَرَّقَ بَيْنَهُمْ حَتَّى يَعْتَدُّونَ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الْقَاسِمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ يَقُولُ اللَّهُ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَالَّذِينَ يُتَوَفَّوْنَ مِنْكُمْ وَيَذَرُونَ أَزْوَاجًا‏}‏ مَا هُنَّ مِنَ الأَزْوَاجِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1252" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Abd al-Malik (unresolved); al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor). The printed governor name and the supplied report of separation and al-Qasim’s objection are retained literally; no external identity or corrected wording is inserted.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said that he had heard al-Qasim ibn Muhammad say that Zayd ibn Abd al-Malik separated some men and their wives who were slave-girls who had borne children to men who had died, because they had married them after one or two menstrual periods. He separated them until they had done an idda of four months and ten days. Al-Qasim ibn Muhammad said, &quot;Glory be to Allah! Allah says in His Book, &#x27;Those of you who die, leaving wives, they are not wives</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1252]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1252" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे कि यज़ीद बिन अब्दुल मलिक ने मर्दों और उन औरतों के दरमियान जो उम्मे-वलद थीं तफ़रीक़ कर दी, और उनके मौला मर गए थे, उन्होंने एक हैज़ या दो हैज़ के बाद निकाह कर लिए थे और हुकुम दिया चार महीने दस दिन इद्दत करने का। तब क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद ने कहा, सुभान अल्लाह! अल्लाह फ़रमाता है जो लोग तुम में से मर जाएँ और बीवियाँ छोड़ जाएँ, वो चार महीने दस दिन इद्दत करें, और उम्मे-वलद बीवियों में दाख़िल नहीं।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1252" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد کہتے تھے کہ یزید بن عبدالملک نے مردوں اور ان عورتوں کے درمیان جو ام ولد تھیں تفریق کر دی اور ان کے مولیٰ مر گئے تھے انہوں نے ایک حیض یا دو حیض کے بعد نکاح کر لئے تھے اور حکم دیا چار مہینے دس دن عدت کرنے کاتب قاسم بن محمد نے کہا سبحان اللہ اللہ فرماتا ہے جو لوگ تم میں سے مر جائیں اور بیبیاں چھوڑ جائیں وہ چار مہینے دس دن عدت کریں اور ام دلد بیبیوں میں داخل نہیں ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the ki Yazid bin Abdul Malik ne mardon aur un auraton ke darmiyan jo umm-e-walad thin tafreeq kar di, aur unke mola mar gaye the, unhone ek haiz ya do haiz ke baad nikah kar liye the aur hukum diya char mahine das din iddat karne ka. Tab Qasim bin Muhammad ne kaha, Subhan Allah! Allah farmata hai jo log tum mein se mar jayen aur bibiyan chhod jayen, woh char mahine das din iddat karen, aur umm-e-walad bibiyon mein dakhil nahi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1252" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1240

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ عِدَّةُ أُمِّ الْوَلَدِ إِذَا تُوُفِّيَ عَنْهَا سَيِّدُهَا حَيْضَةٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1254]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1254" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1242

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1256</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1256 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْعَبْدِ يُطَلِّقُ الأَمَةَ طَلاَقًا لَمْ يَبُتَّهَا فِيهِ لَهُ عَلَيْهَا فِيهِ الرَّجْعَةُ ثُمَّ يَمُوتُ وَهِيَ فِي عِدَّتِهَا مِنْ طَلاَقِهِ إِنَّهَا تَعْتَدُّ عِدَّةَ الأَمَةِ الْمُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا شَهْرَيْنِ وَخَمْسَ لَيَالٍ وَإِنَّهَا إِنْ عَتَقَتْ وَلَهُ عَلَيْهَا رَجْعَةٌ ثُمَّ لَمْ تَخْتَرْ فِرَاقَهُ بَعْدَ الْعِتْقِ حَتَّى يَمُوتَ وَهِيَ فِي عِدَّتِهَا مِنْ طَلاَقِهِ اعْتَدَّتْ عِدَّةَ الْحُرَّةِ الْمُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهَا إِنَّمَا وَقَعَتْ عَلَيْهَا عِدَّةُ الْوَفَاةِ بَعْدَ مَا عَتَقَتْ فَعِدَّتُهَا عِدَّةُ الْحُرَّةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). Only the supplied abbreviated transmission is selected. Its referenced wording is not reconstructed from an adjacent entry.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me the like of that from Malik from Ibn Shihab.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1256]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1256</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1256 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, about a slave who divorced a slave-girl but did not make it absolute, &quot;He can return to her. If he then dies while she is still in the idda from her divorce, she does the idda of a slave- girl whose husband dies, and it is two months and five days. If she has been set free and he can return to her, and she does not choose to separate after she has been set free, and he dies while she is in the idda from the divorce, she does the idda of a free woman whose husband has died, four months and ten days. That is because the idda of widowhood befell her while she was free, so her idda is the idda of a free woman.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That is what is done among us</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सुलैमान बिन यसार कहते थे कि लौंडी का ख़ावंद जब मर जाए तो उसकी इद्दत दो महीने पाँच दिन है। इब्न शिहाब ने भी ऐसा ही कहा है। मालिक ने कहा, जो ग़ुलाम लौंडी को तलाक़-ए-रज़ी दे फिर मर जाए और उसकी औरत इद्दत में हो तो अब दो महीने पाँच दिन तक इद्दत करे। अगर वो लौंडी आज़ाद हो जाए और अपने ख़ावंद से जुदा न होना चाहे यहाँ तक कि ख़ावंद उसका इद्दत में मर जाए, तो अब वो लौंडी मिसल-ए-आज़ाद औरत के चार महीने दस दिन तक इद्दत करे, क्योंकि इद्दत-ए-वफ़ात के बाद आज़ादी उस पर लाज़िम हुई तो मिसल-ए-आज़ाद औरत के करना चाहिए, हमारे नज़दीक यही हुकुम है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار کہتے تھے کہ لونڈی کا خاوند جب مر جائے تو اس کی عدت دو مہینے پانچ دن ہے ۔ ابن شہاب نے بھی ایسا ہی کہا ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے جو غلام لونڈی کو طلاق رجعی دے پھر مرجائے اور اس کی عورت عدت میں ہو تو اب دو مہینے پانچ دن تک عدت کرے۔ اگر وہ لونڈی آزاد ہوجائے اور اپنے خاوند سے جدا نہ ہونا چاہے یہاں تک کہ خاوند اس کا عدت میں مرجائے تو اب وہ لونڈی مثل آزاد عورت کے چار مہینے دس دن تک عدت کرے کیونکہ عدت وفات کے بعد آزادی کے اس پر لازم ہوئی تو مثل آزاد عورت کے کرنا چاہیے ہمارے نزدیک یہی حکم ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1256 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyab aur Sulaiman bin Yasar kehte the ki laundi ka khawand jab mar jaye to uski iddat do mahine paanch din hai. Ibn Shihab ne bhi aisa hi kaha hai. Malik ne kaha, jo ghulam laundi ko talaq-e-raj&#x27;i de phir mar jaye aur uski aurat iddat mein ho to ab do mahine paanch din tak iddat kare. Agar woh laundi azad ho jaye aur apne khawand se juda na hona chahe yahan tak ki khawand uska iddat mein mar jaye, to ab woh laundi misl-e-azad aurat ke char mahine das din tak iddat kare, kyunki iddat-e-wafat ke baad azadi us par lazim hui to misl-e-azad aurat ke karna chahiye, hamare nazdeek yahi hukum hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1257 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يَحْيَى بْنِ حَبَّانَ، عَنِ ابْنِ مُحَيْرِيزٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ دَخَلْتُ الْمَسْجِدَ فَرَأَيْتُ أَبَا سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيَّ فَجَلَسْتُ إِلَيْهِ فَسَأَلْتُهُ عَنِ الْعَزْلِ، فَقَالَ أَبُو سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيُّ خَرَجْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي غَزْوَةِ بَنِي الْمُصْطَلِقِ فَأَصَبْنَا سَبْيًا مِنْ سَبْىِ الْعَرَبِ فَاشْتَهَيْنَا النِّسَاءَ وَاشْتَدَّتْ عَلَيْنَا الْعُزْبَةُ وَأَحْبَبْنَا الْفِدَاءَ فَأَرَدْنَا أَنْ نَعْزِلَ فَقُلْنَا نَعْزِلُ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَيْنَ أَظْهُرِنَا قَبْلَ أَنْ نَسْأَلَهُ ‏.‏ فَسَأَلْنَاهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَا عَلَيْكُمْ أَنْ لاَ تَفْعَلُوا مَا مِنْ نَسَمَةٍ كَائِنَةٍ إِلَى يَوْمِ الْقِيَامَةِ إِلاَّ وَهِيَ كَائِنَةٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah answering the Companions in Abu Said al-Khudris expedition account. The complete field supplies the question to Abu Said, the expedition circumstances, inquiry to the Messenger and the exact supplied reply about conception.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman from Muhammad ibn Yahya ibn Habban that Ibn Muhayriz said, &quot;I went into the mosque and saw Abu Said al-Khudri and so I sat by him and asked him about coitus interruptus. Abu Said al-Khudri said, &#x27;We went out with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, on the expedition to the Banu al-Mustaliq. We took some Arabs prisoner, and we desired the women as celibacy was hard for us. We wanted the ransom, so we wanted to practise coitus interruptus. We said, &#x27;Shall we practise coitus interruptus while the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, is among us before we ask him?&#x27; We asked him about that and he said, &#x27;You don&#x27;t have to not do it. There is no self which is to come into existence up to the Day of Rising but that it will come into existence</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1257]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1257" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1257</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1257 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न मुहय्यरिज़ से रिवायत है कि मैं मस्जिद में गया, वहाँ अबू सईद ख़ुदरी (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को बैठे देखा। मैंने पूछा, अज़्ल (कोइटस इंटरप्टस) दुरुस्त है? उन्होंने कहा, हम रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ ग़ज़वा-ए-बनी मुस्तलिक में गए, वहाँ औरतें काफ़िरों की क़ैद हुईं। हम लोगों को शहवत हुई और मुजर्रदी दुश्वार गुज़री, और यह भी कि हम चाहते थे कि इन औरतों को बेच कर रुपया हासिल करें, इसलिए हमने चाहा कि अज़्ल करें। फिर हमने सोचा कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मौजूद हैं, बग़ैर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछे क्योंकर अज़्ल करें? इसलिए आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया, &#x27;अज़्ल करने में कुछ क़बाहत नहीं, क्योंकि जिस जान को पैदा करना अल्लाह को मंज़ूर है, वो ख़्वाह-मख़्वाह पैदा होगी क़यामत तक&#x27;।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1257" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابن محیریز سے روای تھے کہ میں مسجد میں گیا وہاں ابوسعید خدری کو بیٹھے دیکھا میں نے پوچھا عزل درست ہوئے انہوں نے کہا ہم رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ غزوہ بن مصطلق میں گئے وہاں عورتیں کافروں کی قید ہوئی ہم لوگوں کو شہوت ہوئی اور مجردی دشوار گزری اور یہ بھی کہ ہم چاہتے تھے کہ ان عورتوں کو بیچ کر روپیہ حاصل کریں اس لے ہم نے چاہا کہ عزل کریں پھر ہم نے سوچا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم موجود ہیں بغیر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھے کیونکر عزل کریں اس لئے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا عزل رکنے میں کچھ قباحت نہیں کیونکہ جس جان کو پیدا کرنا اللہ کو منظور ہے وہ خوار مخواہ پیدا ہوگی قیامت تک ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Muhayriz se riwayat hai ki main masjid mein gaya, wahan Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko baithe dekha. Maine poochha, azl (coitus interruptus) durust hai? Unhone kaha, hum Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath ghazwa-e-Bani Mustaliq mein gaye, wahan auratein kafiron ki qaid huin. Hum logon ko shahwat hui aur mujarradi dushwar guzri, aur yeh bhi ki hum chahte the ki in auraton ko bech kar rupiya hasil karen, isliye humne chaha ki azl karen. Phir humne socha ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) maujood hain, baghair Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se poochhe kyonkar azl karen? Isliye Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se poochha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, &#x27;Azl karne mein kuch qabahat nahi, kyunki jis jaan ko paida karna Allah ko manzoor hai, woh khwah-makhwah paida hogi qayamat tak&#x27;.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1257" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1245

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ عَنِ ابْنِ أَفْلَحَ، مَوْلَى أَبِي أَيُّوبَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ عَنْ أُمِّ وَلَدٍ، لأَبِي أَيُّوبَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَعْزِلُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1259" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Ayyubal-Ansari (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;n-Nadr, the mawla of Umar ibn Ubaydullah from Ibn Aflah, the mawla of Abu Ayyub al-Ansari from an umm walad of Abu Ayyubal-Ansari that he practised coitus interruptus</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1259]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1259" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ ضَمْرَةَ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنِ الْحَجَّاجِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ غَزِيَّةَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ جَالِسًا عِنْدَ زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ فَجَاءَهُ ابْنُ قَهْدٍ - رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ - فَقَالَ يَا أَبَا سَعِيدٍ إِنَّ عِنْدِي جَوَارِيَ لِي لَيْسَ نِسَائِي اللاَّتِي أُكِنُّ بِأَعْجَبَ إِلَىَّ مِنْهُنَّ وَلَيْسَ كُلُّهُنَّ يُعْجِبُنِي أَنْ تَحْمِلَ مِنِّي أَفَأَعْزِلُ فَقَالَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ أَفْتِهِ يَا حَجَّاجُ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ يَغْفِرُ اللَّهُ لَكَ إِنَّمَا نَجْلِسُ عِنْدَكَ لِنَتَعَلَّمَ مِنْكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَفْتِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ هُوَ حَرْثُكَ إِنْ شِئْتَ سَقَيْتَهُ وَإِنْ شِئْتَ أَعْطَشْتَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَكُنْتُ أَسْمَعُ ذَلِكَ مِنْ زَيْدٍ فَقَالَ زَيْدٌ صَدَقَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1261" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Damra ibn Said al-Mazini from al-Hajjaj ibn Amr ibn Ghaziya that he was sitting with Zayd ibn Thabit when Ibn Fahd came to him. He was from the Yemen. He said, &quot;Abu Said! I have slave-girls. None of the wives in my keep are more pleasing to me than them, and not all of them please me so much that I want a child by them, shall I then practise coitus interruptus?&quot; Zayd ibn Thabit said, &quot;Give an opinion, Hajjaj!&quot; &quot;I said, &#x27;May Allah forgive you! We sit with you in order to learn from you!&#x27; He said, &#x27;Give an opinion! &#x27;I said, &#x27;She is your field, if you wish, water it, and if you wish, leave it thirsty. I heard that from Zayd.&#x27; Zayd said, &#x27;He has spoken the truth</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1261]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1261" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज्जाज बिन अम्र बिन गज़ियाह बिन साबित बैठे हुए थे के इब्न फ़हाद, जो यमन का रहने वाला था, आया और कहा: ऐ अबू सईद! मेरे पास कुछ लौंडियां (slave women) हैं जो मेरी बीवियों से बेहतर हैं, मगर मैं नहीं चाहता के वो सब हामिला (pregnant) हो जाएं, क्या मैं इनके साथ अज़्ल (withdrawal) करूं? ज़ैद ने हज्जाज से कहा मसला बताओ। हज्जाज ने कहा: अल्लाह तुम्हें बख्शे, हम तो तुम्हारे पास इल्म सीखने आते हैं। ज़ैद ने कहा: बताओ! जब मैं ने कहा वो खेतियां हैं तेरी, तेरा जी चाहे उन में पानी पहुंचा या जी चाहे सूखा रख, मैं ऐसा ही सुना करता था ज़ैद से। ज़ैद ने कहा: सच बोला।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1261" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حجاج بن عمرو بن غزیہ بن ثابت پاس بیٹھے تھے اتنے میں ابن فہد ایک شخص یمن کا رہنے والا آیا اور اکہا اے ابس سعید میرے پاس چند لونڈیاں ہیں جو میری بیبیوں سے بہتر ہیں مکر میں نہ نہیں چاہتا کہ وہ سب حاملہ ہو جائیں کیا میں اس سے عزل کروں زید نے حجاج سے کہا مسئلہ بتاؤ حجاج نے کہا اللہ تمہیں بخشے ہم تو تمہارے پاس علم سکیھنے کو آتے ہیں زید نے کہا بتاؤ جب میں نے کہا وہ کھیتیاں ہیں تیری تیرا جی چاہے ان میں پانی پہنچا یا جی چاہے سوکھا رکھ میں اسیا ہی سنا کرتا تھا زید سے زید نے کہا سچ بولا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hajjaj bin Amr bin Ghaziyah bin Sabit baithe hue the ke Ibn Fahad, jo Yemen ka rehne wala tha, aaya aur kaha: Aye Abu Saeed! Mere paas kuch laundiyan (slave women) hain jo meri biwiyon se behtar hain, magar main nahi chahta ke wo sab hamila (pregnant) ho jayen, kya main inke saath azl (withdrawal) karun? Zaid ne Hajjaj se kaha masla batao. Hajjaj ne kaha: Allah tumhe bakhshe, hum to tumhare paas ilm seekhne aate hain. Zaid ne kaha: Batao! Jab main ne kaha wo khetiyan hain teri, tera ji chahe un mein pani pohancha ya ji chahe sookha rakh, main aisa hi suna karta tha Zaid se. Zaid ne kaha: Sach bola.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1261" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1248

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1262)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1262]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1262</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1262 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، يُقَالُ لَهُ ذَفِيفٌ أَنَّهُ قَالَ سُئِلَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ عَنِ الْعَزْلِ فَدَعَا جَارِيَةً لَهُ فَقَالَ أَخْبِرِيهِمْ ‏.‏ فَكَأَنَّهَا اسْتَحْيَتْ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ هُوَ ذَلِكَ أَمَّا أَنَا فَأَفْعَلُهُ ‏.‏ يَعْنِي أَنَّهُ يَعْزِلُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ يَعْزِلُ الرَّجُلُ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ الْحُرَّةِ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِهَا وَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَعْزِلَ عَنْ أَمَتِهِ بِغَيْرِ إِذْنِهَا وَمَنْ كَانَتْ تَحْتَهُ أَمَةُ قَوْمٍ فَلاَ يَعْزِلُ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِهِمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki that a man called Dhafif said that Ibn Abbas was asked about coitus interruptus. He called a slave-girl of his and said, &quot;Tell them.&quot; She was embarrassed. He said, &quot;It is alright, and I do it myself.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1262]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1262</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1262 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;A man does not practise coitus interruptus with a free woman unless she gives her permission. There is no harm in practising coitus interruptus with a slave-girl without her permission. Someone who has someone else&#x27;s slave-girl as a wife, does not practise coitus interruptus with her unless her people give him permission</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1262</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1262 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ुफ़यफ़ से रिवायत है के इब्न अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल हुआ के अज़्ल करना दुरुस्त है या नहीं। उन्होंने अपनी लौंडी को बुला कर कहा के तुम उन्हें बता दो (लेकिन) उसने शरम की। अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: देख लो, ऐसा ही हुक्म है, मैं तो अज़्ल किया करता हूँ। मालिक ने कहा: आज़ाद औरत से अज़्ल करना बग़ैर उसकी इजाज़त के दुरुस्त नहीं, और अपनी लौंडी से बग़ैर उसकी इजाज़त के दुरुस्त है, और पराई लौंडी से उसके मालिक की इजाज़त लेना ज़रूरी है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ذفیف سے روایت ہے کہ ابن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے سوال ہوا عزل کرنا درست ہے یا نہیں انہوں نے اپنی لونڈی کو بلا کر کہا تو ان کو بتا دے اس نے شرم کیا عبداللہ بن عباس نے کہا دیکھ لو ایسا ہی حکم ہے میں تو عزل کیا کرتا ہوں ۔ کہا مالک نے آزاد عورت سے عزل کرنا بغیر اس کی اجازت کے درست نہیں اور اپنی لونڈی سے بغیر اس کی اجازت کے درست ہے اور پرائی لونڈی سے اس کے مالک کی اجازت لینا ضروری ہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zufayf se riwayat hai ke Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se sawal hua ke azl karna durust hai ya nahi. Unhone apni laundi ko bula kar kaha ke tum unhe bata do (lekin) usne sharam ki. Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Dekh lo, aisa hi hukm hai, main to azl kiya karta hoon. Malik ne kaha: Azad aurat se azl karna baghair uski ijazat ke durust nahi, aur apni laundi se baghair uski ijazat ke durust hai, aur parayi laundi se uske maalik ki ijazat lena zaroori hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1262" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1249

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1263)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ زَيْنَبَ بِنْتِ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ هَذِهِ الأَحَادِيثَ الثَّلاَثَةَ، قَالَتْ زَيْنَبُ دَخَلْتُ عَلَى أُمِّ حَبِيبَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم حِينَ تُوُفِّيَ أَبُوهَا أَبُو سُفْيَانَ بْنُ حَرْبٍ فَدَعَتْ أُمُّ حَبِيبَةَ بِطِيبٍ فِيهِ صُفْرَةٌ خَلُوقٌ أَوْ غَيْرُهُ فَدَهَنَتْ بِهِ جَارِيَةً ثُمَّ مَسَحَتْ بِعَارِضَيْهَا ثُمَّ قَالَتْ وَاللَّهِ مَا لِي بِالطِّيبِ مِنْ حَاجَةٍ غَيْرَ أَنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَحِلُّ لاِمْرَأَةٍ تُؤْمِنُ بِاللَّهِ وَالْيَوْمِ الآخِرِ أَنْ تُحِدَّ عَلَى مَيْتٍ فَوْقَ ثَلاَثِ لَيَالٍ إِلاَّ عَلَى زَوْجٍ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1263]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1263" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm from Humayd ibn Nafi that Zaynab bint Abi Salama related these three traditions to him. Zaynab said, &quot;I visited Umm Habiba, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, when her father Abu Sufyan ibn Harb had died. Umm Habiba called for a yellowy perfume perhaps khaluq or something else. She rubbed the perfume first on a slave-girl and she then wiped it on the sides of her face and said, &#x27;By Allah! I have no need of perfume but I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &#x27;It is not halal for a woman who trusts in Allah and the Last Day to abstain from adornment in mourning for someone who has died, for more than three nights, except for four months and ten days for a husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1263]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1263" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हुमैद बिन नाफ़ी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के ज़ैनब बिंत अबी सलमा ने तीन हदीसें बयान कीं। एक ये के मैं उम्मे हबीबा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास गई जो बीवी थीं (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की, जब उनके बाप अबू सुफ़ियान बिन हर्ब मरे थे, तो उम्मे हबीबा ने खुशबू मंगवाई जिसमें दिया मिला हुआ था, वो खुशबू एक लौंडी के लगा कर अपने गालों पर लगा ली और कहा: क़सम अल्लाह की, मुझे खुशबू की एहतियाज नहीं, मगर मैं ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) फ़रमाते थे: जो औरत ईमान लाए अल्लाह पर और पिछले दिन (क़यामत) पर, उसको दुरुस्त नहीं के किसी मुर्दे पर तीन दिन से ज़्यादा सोग करे, सिवाये ख़ावंद के के उस पर चार महीने दस दिन तक सोग करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1263]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حمید بن نافع رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ زبیب بنت ابی سلمہ نے تین حدیثیں ان سے بیان کیں ایک تو یہ کہ میں ام حبیبہ کے پاس گئی جو بی بی تھیں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی جب ان کے باپ ابوسفیان بن حرب مرے تھے تو ام حبیبہ نے خوشبو منگوائی جس روز دی ملی ہوئی تھی وہ خوشبو ایک لونڈی کے لگا کر اپنے کلوں پر لگالی اور کہا کہ قسم اللہ کی مجھ خوشبو کی احتیاج نہیں مگر میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے سنا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم فرماتے تھے جو عورت ایمان لائے اللہ پر اور پچھلے دن پر اس کو درست نہیں کہ کسی مردے پر تین دن تک زیادہ سوگ کرے سواے خاوند کے کہ اس پر چار مہینے دس دن تک سوگ کرے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1263]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1263" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Humayd bin Nafi (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Zaynab bint Abi Salama ne teen hadeesein bayan ki. Ek ye ke main Umme Habiba (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas gayi jo biwi thin (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki, jab unke baap Abu Sufyan bin Harb mare the, to Umme Habiba ne khushbu mangwayi jisme diya mila hua tha, wo khushbu ek laundi ke laga kar apne galon par laga li aur kaha: Qasam Allah ki, mujhe khushbu ki ehtiyaaj nahi, magar main ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) farmate the: Jo aurat imaan laye Allah par aur pichle din (Qayamat) par, usko durust nahi ke kisi murde par teen din se zyada sog kare, siwaye khawand ke ke us par chaar mahine das din tak sog kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1263]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1263" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1250

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1264)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَتْ زَيْنَبُ ثُمَّ دَخَلْتُ عَلَى زَيْنَبَ بِنْتِ جَحْشٍ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم حِينَ تُوُفِّيَ أَخُوهَا فَدَعَتْ بِطِيبٍ فَمَسَّتْ مِنْهُ ثُمَّ قَالَتْ وَاللَّهِ مَا لِي بِالطِّيبِ حَاجَةٌ غَيْرَ أَنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَحِلُّ لاِمْرَأَةٍ تُؤْمِنُ بِاللَّهِ وَالْيَوْمِ الآخِرِ تُحِدُّ عَلَى مَيْتٍ فَوْقَ ثَلاَثِ لَيَالٍ إِلاَّ عَلَى زَوْجٍ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaynab said, &quot;I went to the house of Zaynab bint Jahsh, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, when her brother had died. She called for perfume and put some on and said, &#x27;By Allah! I have no need of perfume, but I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &#x27;It is not halal for a woman who trusts in Allah and the Last Day to abstain from adornment in mourning for someone who has died for more than three nights, except for four months and ten days for a husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैनब ने कहा: मैं ज़ैनब बिंत जहश के पास गई जो बीवी थीं (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की, जब उनके भाई मरे थे, उन्होंने खुशबू मंगा कर लगाई और कहा: क़सम अल्लाह की, मुझे खुशबू की हाजत नहीं, मगर मैं ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) फ़रमाते थे: जो औरत ईमान लाए अल्लाह पर और पिछले दिन पर, उसको दुरुस्त नहीं के सोग करे किसी मुर्दे पर तीन रोज़ से ज़्यादा, मगर ख़ावंद पर चार महीने दस दिन तक सोग करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زینب نے کہا میں زینب بن جحش پاس جو بی بی تھیں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی گئی جب ان کے بھائی مر گئے تھے انہوں نے خوشومنگا کر لگائی اور کہا قسم اللہ کی مجھے خوشبو کی حاجت نہیں مگر میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سنا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم فرماتے تھے جو عورت ایمان لائے اللہ پر اور پچھلے دن پر اس کو درست نہیں کہ سوگ کرے کسی مردے پر تین روز سے زیادہ مگر خاوند پر چار مہینے دس دن تک سوگ کرے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaynab ne kaha: Main Zaynab bint Jahsh ke paas gayi jo biwi thin (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki, jab unke bhai mare the, unhone khushbu manga kar lagayi aur kaha: Qasam Allah ki, mujhe khushbu ki haajat nahi, magar main ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) farmate the: Jo aurat imaan laye Allah par aur pichle din par, usko durust nahi ke sog kare kisi murde par teen roz se zyada, magar khawand par chaar mahine das din tak sog kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
